# sw.sword integration layer index (generated)

Generated by `forge/tools/build_integration_index.py` from sw.sword **`master` @ `51bdc0ec9e`** (2026-11-05). Callers read from sw.models `master` @ `d2f78fafcd`; sw.soult `NavySmallUpdates` @ `c1b3ba6595`.

**Stale check:** if `git -C C:/MASARepo/sw.sword rev-parse --short=10 HEAD` differs from the commit above, treat this file as a hint only and confirm with grep (line numbers drift first). **Grep this file, never read it whole.**

Scope: `data/app-data/resources/Integration.lua` + `integration/*.lua`. One row per `integration.X` name, grep `^| .integration.X. ` (the dots stand for the backticks); search words in the Summary column to find an existing function. **Kind**: function, alias (= another name), startStopAction, table, value, constructor key (a key of the `integration` table built in `Integration.lua`, e.g. `ontology`: `integration.ontology.types.<key>` is the current role's type map, see `docs/sw.models/ontology_index.md` section 6). **Summary**: first sentence of the LDoc `---` block above the definition (empty = undocumented). **Brain calls**: `DEC_*` tokens and registered brain functions in the body (direct only). **Kernel starters**: brain functions registered with `StartAction<` reached through the body, Lua helpers and other `integration.*` (same closure as `docs/sw.models/action_index.md` section 4). **Layer / C++**: references inside the layer (definitions excluded) / in kernel C++ (`src/libraries/simulation`). **sw.models users**: distinct files (dia5 entity names; `model:` prefix outside ada), first 5 shown; **Soult**: distinct sw.soult files. **Overridden by**: model Lua assigning the same name (models load after the layer, so their definition wins, except where a load-time local cached the old one: section 3.2). Lua references are textual: names built at runtime are invisible.

## 2. Model-side definitions of `integration.*`

Assignments to `integration.X` in model Lua. **Override** = the layer defines the same name; **model-only** = the layer does not (the model extends the namespace).

| Repo | Name | File:line | Override? |
|---|---|---|---|

## 3. Anomalies

### 3.1 Defined more than once in the layer (1)

Names with two or more function / alias / startStopAction definitions (plain value assignments are not counted). The definition that runs last wins (load order: `Integration.lua` includes; a redefinition inside a function applies only once that function runs).

| Name | Definitions |
|---|---|
| `integration.isFlying` | `integration/DestroyableIntegration.lua`:282 (function), `integration/EquipmentIntegration.lua`:1375 (function) |

### 3.2 Overridden by a model and cached as a load-time local (0)

`local f = integration.f` keeps the value it had when that file loaded: a layer file caching it keeps the layer version even after the model override.

| Name | Overridden by | Cached in |
|---|---|---|

### 3.3 Referenced in sw.models Lua, defined nowhere (0)

Not defined in the layer nor by any sw.models Lua assignment found: dead code, a typo, or a name built at runtime / defined by another mechanism. Confirm by grep before acting.

| Name | Used in |
|---|---|

### 3.4 Never referenced (292)

Defined in the layer, with no reference in the layer, kernel C++, sw.models or sw.soult Lua (on the branches read). Candidates for cleanup, or entry points called by name from elsewhere (exercise scripts, other models).
